*Fatima Azzahraa Amarcha,Lahcen Hassine,Rachid Saadane,Mohamed Rahouti,Rachid Ahl Laamara,Abdallah Slaoui,Hany S. khalifa*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出混合K-means量子启发进化算法(HKQEA)，求解灾后UAV作为空中基站的联合激活与连续空间部署问题，在满足覆盖与最小间距约束下最小化UAV数量，最优方案仅需8架UAV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾后地面通信基础设施失效，需快速部署UAV空中基站恢复无线连接。核心挑战在于连续空间中联合决定UAV激活数量与位置，同时满足覆盖和最小间距约束，并尽量少用UAV。

Method: 提出HKQEA，融合四要素：K-means引导初始化、校准的惩罚式可行性目标、非精英进化搜索、量子启发学习更新。与NSGA-II、PSO及HKQEA精英变体对比，50次独立运行评估。

Result: 50次运行中，HKQEA最优全可行解为8架UAV；平均覆盖率98.94%、非重叠率99.94%、最小距离满足率99.68%。对比NSGA-II、PSO和精英变体，在探索、收敛与可行性保持间取得更优平衡。采购级分析显示UAV从10架减至8架可降低20%硬件数量，对应约25%的简化节省比。

Conclusion: HKQEA能为受约束部署问题提供可靠的可行性保持与资源高效方案，在灾后通信恢复中具有应用潜力。

Abstract: In post-disaster environments, the failure of terrestrial communication infrastructure necessitates the rapid deployment of unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) as aerial base stations to restore wireless connectivity. This paper addresses the joint UAV activation-and-placement problem in continuous space, with the objective of minimizing the number of deployed UAVs while satisfying coverage and minimum-separation constraints. To solve this problem, we propose a Hybrid K-means Quantum-Inspired Evolutionary Algorithm (HKQEA) that combines K-means-guided initialization, a calibrated penalty-based feasibility objective, non-elitist evolutionary search, and a quantum-inspired learning update. Experimental results over 50 independent runs show that HKQEA attains a best fully feasible solution with 8 UAVs, while achieving average values of 98.94\% for coverage, 99.94\% for non-overlap, and 99.68\% for minimum-distance satisfaction. Comparative evaluation against standard Non-dominated Sorting Genetic Algorithm II (NSGA-II), Particle Swarm Optimization algorithm (PSO) and an elitist variant of HKQEA further shows that the proposed method provides a more favorable balance among exploration, convergence behavior, and reliable feasibility preservation in constrained deployment problems. An illustrative procurement-level cost analysis also indicates that reducing the fleet from 10 UAVs to 8 can yield a 20\% reduction in hardware count, corresponding to a simplified savings ratio of 25\% for the studied deployment setting. These results demonstrate the potential of the proposed framework for resource-efficient post-disaster communication restoration.

</details>


### [368] [Scaled Hippocampus-inspired Neural Networks on Neuromorphic Memristive Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16429)
*Joseph A. Kilgore,Jeffrey D. Kopsick,Zahin Ahmed,Giorgio A. Ascoli,Gina C. Adam*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 该工作借鉴啮齿类CA3海马，构建首个具神经元多样性与生物真实静息态动力学的脉冲神经网络，并成功映射到忆阻器硬件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海马具有结构多样性、稀疏通信、稳健动力学与高能效，结合新兴硬件有望催生新型计算能力。

Method: 采用4目标函数降尺度方法，构建含179个Izhikevich神经元、3种类型、17,996个突触的小规模CA3网络，并用贪心算法映射到FPGA/忆阻器平台（18,316个忆阻器）。

Result: 小规模网络保持与大规模网络相似的静息态动力学；忆阻器噪声使其呈现连续周期行为，优于模拟硬件。

Conclusion: 展示了生物真实算法与新兴硬件结合用于神经形态计算的潜力。

Abstract: The hippocampus, a key brain region for learning and memory, exhibits rich structural diversity, sparse communication, and robust dynamics with incredible energy efficiency. It offers promising insights for novel computing capabilities, particularly when co-designed with emerging hardware technologies. In this work, we draw inspiration from the rodent CA3 hippocampal subregion to develop the first spiking neural network with neuronal diversity and biologically-realistic resting state dynamics demonstrated on memristor hardware. We propose a network downscaling methodology utilizing a 4-prong objective function and demonstrate a small-scale CA3-inspired network with 179 Izhikevich-modeled neurons, 3 neuronal types and 17,996 synapses with similar resting-state dynamics as the orders-of-magnitude larger full-scale network. The small-scale network is mapped to an FPGA/memristor platform using a greedy algorithm and 18,316 memristors. Benefiting from memristor noise, the hardware implementation shows continuous periodic behavior, outperforming simulated hardware. This work showcases the potential of biologically-realistic algorithms on emerging hardware for neuromorphic computing.

</details>


### [369] [LLMDE: A Large Language Model-Driven Differential Evolution Algorithm for Portfolio Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16846)
*Rong Chai,Vaclav Snasel,Xiaopeng Wang,Seyedali Mirjalili,Crina Grosan*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出LLMDE算法，用大语言模型驱动差分进化，减少对手工超参数设计的依赖，并在基准测试和投资组合优化任务上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统差分进化算法依赖手工设计的超参数（变异策略与控制参数），调参困难且泛化性差，希望借助LLM降低这一依赖。

Method: 采用提示工程策略，让LLM根据优化反馈动态选择变异策略并配置控制参数；在CEC2022基准上评测；并通过因子分析与K-means聚类选股，进而用LLMDE求解带预算和最低预期收益约束的CVaR投资组合优化问题。

Result: LLMDE在CEC2022基准上表现具有竞争力，并在复杂约束优化任务中持续生成高质量解。

Conclusion: 结果证明了将LLM嵌入元启发式算法的可行性，为LLM辅助的先进优化技术设计提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: This study proposes a Large Language Model-Driven Differential Evolution (LLMDE) algorithm to reduce the reliance on handcrafted hyperparameter design. The proposed algorithm leverages a prompt engineering strategy, allowing large language models (LLMs) to dynamically select mutation strategies and configure control parameters guided by optimization feedback, thus enhancing the performance of the DE algorithm. We evaluate the performance of LLMDE on the CEC2022 benchmark suite, comparing it with standard DE and representative metaheuristics. Furthermore, we employ factor analysis and K-means clustering for stock selection, and then apply LLMDE to solve the Conditional Value at Risk (CVaR) portfolio optimization problem using the selected stocks, subject to budget and minimum expected return constraints. Experimental results demonstrate that LLMDE achieves competitive performance on the benchmark suite while continuously generating high-quality solutions for complex constrained optimization tasks. These outcomes successfully demonstrate the viability of embedding LLMs within metaheuristics, paving a promising path toward the design of advanced LLM-assisted optimization techniques.

</details>


### [370] [Bio-Inspired Palette Evolution in Indirectly Encoded Substrates: Timescale Compatibility Shapes Activation Function Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17067)
*Romain Claret,Michael O'Neill,Paul Cotofrei,Kilian Stoffel*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文把"如何让进化发现节点激活函数"作为元学习问题，设计13种策略（11种生物启发+基线+oracle）动态修改激活函数集合，在3000+次运行中验证：生物启发策略达到基线解率但收敛快达2倍，策略排名随问题类型反转，且成功关键在于机制时间尺度与评估窗口匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 间接编码神经网络可为各节点分配不同激活函数，但合适函数难预知：仅含单调函数时parity等问题无解，而全包含调色板又不如精选集合。因此需解决"进化应如何发现使用哪些函数"的问题。

Method: 将问题建模为元学习，设计13种策略（11种生物适应机制+基线+oracle）在进化中修改可用激活函数集合；每种策略转化为进化算子，如昼夜节律启发的振荡门控按固定调度增删函数，免疫启发的克隆选择永久保护与适应度稳定相关的函数。在parity与非parity问题上进行3000+次运行，先单独进化激活调色板，再在更难问题上共同进化每节点聚合调色板。

Result: 新种子独立复现确认存在稳定的高可靠性层级，Circadian保持排名第一。生物启发策略解率与调参基线相当，但收敛最快可达2倍，Circadian将总计算量减半。策略排名在不同问题类型间反转，无单一策略主导所有领域。成功主要受时间尺度匹配影响：机制特征时间尺度与进化评估窗口匹配者稳定优于过慢者。

Conclusion: 实用指南是让机制的时间尺度匹配评估预算。重新缩放最慢策略可绕过振荡障碍：全部九个解以非振荡激活搭配min或max聚合解决parity问题。

Abstract: Indirectly encoded neural networks can assign different activation functions to individual nodes, but the right functions are rarely known in advance. When the available set contains only standard monotonic functions, problems like parity become unsolvable, yet an all-inclusive palette underperforms a curated one. How should evolution discover which functions to use? We address this as a meta-learning problem, designing 13 strategies (11 inspired by biological adaptation mechanisms, plus baseline and oracle controls) that modify the set of available activation functions during evolution. Each strategy translates a biological principle into an evolutionary operator: for example, circadian-inspired oscillatory gating cycles functions in and out of the palette on a fixed schedule, while immune-inspired Clonal Selection permanently protects functions that consistently correlate with fitness. We evaluate all strategies across more than 3,000 runs on parity and non-parity problems, first evolving the activation palette alone, then co-evolving a per-node aggregation palette on harder problems; an independent replication with new seeds confirms a stable high-reliability tier, with Circadian holding its top rank. Bio-inspired strategies match the solve rate of a tuned baseline but converge up to twice as fast, with Circadian halving total compute. Strategy rankings reverse across problem types, with no strategy dominating all domains. Strategy success is largely shaped by timescale compatibility: strategies whose characteristic timescale matches the evolutionary evaluation window consistently outperform those that operate too slowly. The practical guideline: match the mechanism's timescale to the evaluation budget. Rescaling the slowest strategy bypasses the oscillatory barrier entirely: all nine solutions solve parity with non-oscillatory activations paired with min or max aggregation.

</details>


### [371] [Machine Zygote: Causal Biparental Heredity Before Learning in a Germline--Soma Artificial Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17300)
*Lyes Saad Saoud*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Machine Zygote 架构，验证人工代理在出生前（无学习）能否表现出可测量的双亲遗传效应，并证明这种依赖是因果性的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工个体发生、发育编码等已有研究，但核心问题未解：新生人工代理能否在无学习前表现出可测量的双亲遗传，且该依赖能否被因果隔离而非仅由亲子相似性推断。

Method: 构建"种系-体细胞"计算架构：两套亲代种系独立突变并重组为合子，参数化发育一个初始通用的八模块体细胞，随后冻结评估、不进行学习。开展预注册的 4x4 双列杂交（640 后代）及匹配背景干预（n=60），并做准静态无动力学消融。

Result: 6 个行为性状中 5 个显示显著母系与父系依赖（Holm 校正后）；亲代与交互成分占 5 个主要性状建模方差的 36-53%。干预中替换单亲种系引起表型偏移超过同亲重突变对照。重组对速度和步态频率产生超亲后代。发育依赖假说未获支持：无动力学消融保留均值分布但改变亲代方差结构。

Conclusion: 支持该模拟中存在的因果性双亲出生前遗传，但不支持"循环发育动力学为必要"的更强主张；不涉及物理遗传、生物基因或自主进化。贡献是干预中心框架与可复现基准。

Abstract: Artificial ontogeny, developmental encodings, robot reproduction, and inherited controllers are established research directions, yet a narrower question remains: can a newborn artificial agent exhibit measurable biparental heredity before learning, and can that dependence be isolated causally rather than inferred only from parent-offspring resemblance? We introduce Machine Zygote, a computational germline-soma architecture designed to test this question. Two parental germlines are independently mutated and recombined into a zygote that parameterizes development of an initially generic eight-module soma, which is then frozen and evaluated without learning. A preregistered 4 x 4 diallel of 640 offspring shows significant dam and sire dependence for five of six behavioral traits after Holm correction, with parental and interaction components accounting for 36-53 percent of modeled variance across five principal traits. In matched-background interventions (n=60), substituting one parental germline while holding recombination and stochastic background fixed causes phenotype shifts exceeding a same-parent re-mutation control for five of six traits for both parental channels. Recombination also yields excess transgressive offspring for speed and gait frequency. A preregistered developmental-dependence hypothesis is not supported: a quasistatic no-dynamics ablation preserves the mean phenotype distribution while altering parental variance structure. Thus the study supports causal biparental pre-learning heredity in this simulation, but not the stronger claim that recurrent developmental dynamics are necessary. It does not establish physical heredity, biological genetics, or autonomous evolution. The contribution is an intervention-centered framework and reproducible benchmark for separating heredity, development, stochastic variation, and post-birth learning.

</details>


### [372] [A Spatiotemporal Extension of the Neuromorphic DBSCAN Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17357)
*Charles P. Rizzo,James S. Plank*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 该工作将神经形态DBSCAN的"flat"和"systolic"两种实现扩展为时空版本，以更好利用事件传感器数据的时空特性，并提供开源实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件传感器数据具有时空特性，此前神经形态DBSCAN实现未充分利用这一特性，因此需要时空扩展。

Method: 在既有"flat"（重吞吐）和"systolic"（以时间换空间、更省硬件）两种构造基础上，提出其时空扩展，并讨论在硬件资源受限时的部分/分段实现，进一步以时间换空间。

Result: 所有网络构造均以开源实现提供；分段/部分实现可在资源受限时进一步节省空间。

Conclusion: 通过时空扩展与分段实现，神经形态DBSCAN能更契合事件传感器数据并适应硬件约束。

Abstract: DBSCAN is an algorithm that denoises and clusters data. In prior work, we implemented the DBSCAN algorithm neuromorphically, introducing two constructions termed ``flat'' and ``systolic''. The ``flat'' construction prioritizes throughput, while the ``systolic'' construction trades time for space resulting in a smaller, more hardware-friendly architecture at the cost of throughput. In this work, we offer spatiotemporal extensions of these two constructions to better leverage the spatiotemporal nature of event sensor data. Moreover, as in our prior work, we discuss partial or segmented implementations that further leverage time for space when hardware resources are constrained. All network constructions are provided as open-source implementations.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [373] [Evaluating Brand Retrieval and Ranking in Large Language Model Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16304)
*Edward Malthouse,Kun-Yu Lee,Jing Yang,Sanchary Pal,Xueyan Feng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个评估大语言模型开放式品牌推荐的框架，将竞争集独立于模型输出定义，并通过重复采样估计推荐概率与显著性，用BRP@k和MRR@k衡量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM用于产品推荐时，评估方式与传统信息检索和推荐系统不同：它可以在没有显式候选集的情况下生成推荐，且对同一查询的重复响应会产生不同品牌和排序，现有评估方法难以应对这种随机性和开放性。

Method: 构建一个评估开放式LLM品牌推荐的框架：独立于模型输出定义竞争集，通过重复采样估计推荐流行度与显著性；使用品牌推荐概率BRP@k和平均倒数排名MRR@k作为操作化指标；在六个LLM、五个产品类别上进行类别查询、需求查询和诊断性定位探测。

Result: 仅类别查询显示：成熟品牌大量被遗漏，且推荐显著性缺乏遵循传统品牌知名度的证据；显著性反而与更广泛的市场可见性信号相关，尤其是搜索兴趣和在线品牌讨论。需求查询表明，用户目标和约束的上下文化会改变被检索的品牌；诊断性定位探测显示，普通推荐中被遗漏的品牌在提供独特线索时仍可条件性检索到。

Conclusion: 应将LLM推荐评估视为随机检索与排序过程，而不是依据单个生成列表。作者提供开源软件和数据以支持对LLM生成品牌推荐的可复现评估。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for product recommendation, but evaluating their recommendations presents challenges that differ from conventional information retrieval and recommender systems. LLMs can generate recommendations without an explicit candidate set, and repeated responses to the same query can produce different brands and rankings. We introduce a framework for evaluating open-ended LLM brand recommendations that defines the competitive set independently of model outputs and estimates recommendation prevalence and prominence through repeated sampling. We operationalize these constructs using Brand Recommendation Probability (BRP@$k$) and Mean Reciprocal Rank (MRR@$k$), and apply the framework to six LLMs across five product categories. Category-only queries reveal substantial omission of established brands and limited evidence that recommendation prominence follows conventional brand popularity. Instead, prominence is associated with broader marketplace-visibility signals, particularly search interest and online brand conversation. Needs-based queries show that contextualizing users' goals and constraints changes which brands are retrieved, while diagnostic positioning probes demonstrate that brands omitted from ordinary recommendations can remain conditionally retrievable when distinctive cues are supplied. These findings highlight the need to evaluate LLM recommendation as a stochastic retrieval-and-ranking process rather than from individual generated lists. We provide open-source software and data to support reproducible evaluation of LLM-generated brand recommendations.

</details>


### [374] [Where Post-Training Quantization Breaks Text Embedders: A Measured Map Across Four Embedder Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16391)
*Hyojung Han*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文直接检验"保护嵌入表、按模块敏感度分配比特、偏好排序损失"这三条来自LLM量化的启发式在检索嵌入模型上的可迁移性，发现它们在四类架构、五种检查点上普遍失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 权重-only后训练量化是压缩检索嵌入模型最廉价的手段，但主流建议（保护嵌入表、按模块敏感度分配比特、用排序感知目标替代权重重构）是从LLM量化直接照搬的，缺乏在检索嵌入模型上的直接验证。

Method: 对来自4个架构家族的5个检查点做权重量化，在多种比特宽度与分组大小的网格上扫描，并分别在每一宽度下隔离嵌入、注意力与前馈模块进行消融分析。

Result: 三条启发式均失效：嵌入表并非主导性保护对象（尽管常为最大张量）；模块敏感度排序不可迁移（INT4/g16模块间差异过小，INT3排序依赖家族且损伤不可加，INT2下相同重构误差对应1.3%–65.9%的保留率）；便宜的权重重构代理只适合筛选统一位宽，不适合选择保护张量，其跨网格的"强表现"实为扩展范围伪影。量化后的109M蒸馏学生在INT3下以68.4MB取得78.04 NDCG@10，在体积与质量上均胜过其0.6B教师的极端PTQ版本（297.9MB、64.46），但仅限其蒸馏任务内。

Conclusion: 面向LLM的权重量化启发式不能直接迁移到检索嵌入模型；尺寸均按实际文件字节数计，测量仓库保留了每个文件的字节来源。

Abstract: Weight-only post-training quantization is the cheapest way to shrink a retrieval embedder, and the received advice for applying it -- protect the embedding table, allocate bits by module sensitivity, prefer a ranking-aware objective over weight reconstruction -- was carried into LLM quantization largely intact. We test that advice on retrieval embedders directly, quantizing five checkpoints from four architecture families across a grid of bit widths and group sizes, and isolating the embedding, attention and feed-forward blocks at each width.
  Every heuristic fails to transfer as stated. The embedding table never emerges as the dominant isolated protection priority in any family, despite being the largest tensor in several of them. Module sensitivity does not survive as a transferable ordering: at INT4/g16 the spread between modules is too small to allocate against, at INT3 the ordering becomes family-dependent and joint damage stops being the sum of its parts, and at INT2 comparable reconstruction error accompanies retention ranging from 1.3 to 65.9 percent of full precision. A cheap reconstruction proxy is useful for screening uniform bit widths but substantially less reliable for choosing which tensors to protect; its apparent strength across the whole grid is a range-extension artifact.
  A distilled 109M student at INT3 holds 78.04 NDCG@10 in 68.4 MB and dominates the extreme-PTQ arm of its own 0.6B teacher, 297.9 MB at 64.46, on both size and quality -- but only inside the task it was distilled for. Sizes are byte counts of files that exist rather than arithmetic estimates, and the measurement repository carries the byte provenance for every one of them.

</details>


### [375] [Balancing Trial and Reorder: A Hybrid Sequential Transformer-GBDT Ranker for On-Demand Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16407)
*Marcel Kurovski,Attila Nagy,Steffen Klempau,Aleksandr Fedintsev*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: UVR是Wolt部署的生产级统一店铺排序系统，用Transformer编码器做用户序列建模＋GBDT排序器，替代4个独立模型，通过标签平滑与偏向新店的样本加权，在三个连续A/B测试中提升新店试用率并简化服务栈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配送平台上的个性化店铺排序极大影响用户发现与下单。与纯数字域不同，候选店铺是本地化的，受实时可用性和配送运营约束。核心建模矛盾在于：既要展示新店以促进试用，又要保持有复购意图会话的排序质量。

Method: UVR将双向Transformer编码器用于用户序列建模，与整合上下文、用户和店铺特征的GBDT排序器配对。在全国所有店铺和域上训练，推理时施加本地配送约束。用标签平滑和偏向试用的样本加权引导模型倾向新店，用一个统一系统替代原先四个排序模型（三个餐厅＋一个零售）。

Result: 离线试用MRR较生产提升+12%~+30%，但六个国家中五个复购MRR回退；核心线上指标Global CVR统计上不变。三次连续A/B测试：V1带来+5.5%新店试用率和+0.16% Global CVR；V2再增+0.45%新店试用率；V3跨域统一再增+1.31%零售新店试用率，并带来显著增量订单价值和更简化的服务栈。

Conclusion: UVR证明在生产配送平台中，可以用单一统一排序系统在保持核心线上指标的同时提升新店试用，并跨餐厅与零售域泛化，带来可观增量GMV和更简单的服务架构。

Abstract: On a delivery platform, personalized store ranking greatly influences what users find and order. Unlike digital-only domains, candidate stores are local and bound by real-time availability and delivery operations. One central modeling tension is between surfacing new stores for trial and preserving ranking quality for sessions with reorder intent. We present Universal Venue Ranker (UVR), a production system deployed at Wolt that pairs a bidirectional transformer encoder for sequential user modeling with a GBDT ranker integrating contextual, user, and store features. Trained across all stores and domains of a country while enforcing local delivery constraints at inference, UVR replaces four previously separate ranking models (three for restaurants, one for retail) with a single unified system. Label smoothing and trial-biased sample weighting steer the model toward new stores, lifting offline trial MRR by +12% to +30% over production while regressing reorder MRR in five of six countries. These regressions leave Global CVR, our core online metric, which blends trial and reorder sessions, statistically unchanged. We validate UVR in three consecutive A/B tests, the first two across Wolt's largest operating markets and the third spanning all operating countries and both domains. UVR V1 delivers +5.5% Merchant Trial Rate and +0.16% Global CVR over the previous production ranker; V2 adds a further +0.45% Merchant Trial Rate on top; and V3, our cross-domain unification of the restaurant and retail rankers, adds a further +1.31% Retail Merchant Trial Rate, together accounting for substantial incremental gross order value and a materially simplified serving stack.

</details>


### [376] [PCap: Personalized Retrieval-Stage Diversity Capping in Facebook Marketplace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16452)
*Guangchao Yuan,Janis Fuh,Christopher Choate,Xun Tang,Wenqi Zhu,Chengyi Zhang,Pavan Kumar Paalya Chandrashekar,Jiang Han,Jiangyuan Li,Hongyan Wang,Shuting Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: PCap 是一个在检索阶段引入用户级多样性约束的个性化框架，通过熵评分、用户分桶和个性化类别上限提升 Facebook Marketplace 的多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升 Facebook Marketplace 的多样性，在检索阶段实现用户级（个性化）多样性控制。

Method: 用 Shannon 熵评分建模个体多样性偏好，将用户分入多样性桶，在多源候选检索中应用个性化类别上限；用自动化在线优化方法 Parameter Tuning Sequence 处理每桶上限的高维参数空间。

Result: 大规模在线实验表明 PCap 显著改善了用户浏览体验（参与度指标提升）。

Conclusion: 为在工业检索系统中整合个性化多样性提供了实用经验。

Abstract: We propose a personalized capping framework (PCap) to improve the diversity in Facebook Marketplace by introducing user-level diversity constraints at the retrieval stage. PCap models individual diversity preferences using Shannon entropy-based scoring, segments users into diversity buckets, and applies personalized category caps during multi-source candidate retrieval. To navigate the high-dimensional parameter space of per-bucket caps, we leverage an automated online optimization method called Parameter Tuning Sequence. Large-scale online experiments demonstrate that PCap significantly improves users' browsing experience shown in engagement metrics. This work provides practical insights into integrating personalized diversity into industrial retrieval systems.

</details>


### [377] [Predicting Partial Answer Quality and Utility in Agentic Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16453)
*Fangzheng Tian,Debasis Ganguly,Craig Macdonald*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轨迹内探测框架，用于研究Agentic RAG多跳问答中的中间答案状态，并基于此预测部分答案质量与效用，实现早停以降低约11%迭代次数同时保留约98%答案质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agentic RAG评估多关注端到端结果，缺乏对生成过程中模型答案状态如何变化的可见性。作者希望探究迭代检索-推理过程中中间答案质量的演化规律，以理解后期迭代是否真正带来收益。

Method: 提出轨迹内探测框架：每次检索-推理迭代后强制模型停止推理并生成中间答案，据此定义两个迭代级指标——部分答案质量（每轮中间答案质量）与部分效用（跨迭代的质量变化）。随后构建两个预测任务（部分答案质量预测和部分效用预测），从迭代内、迭代间、查询-迭代三个视角提取轨迹信号进行监督学习。

Result: 在多跳QA基准上发现：部分答案质量常在自然终止前即趋于平台期，许多后期迭代仅带来微小提升。部分答案质量比部分效用更易预测，监督模型在质量预测上Pearson r超过0.43。使用预测的质量与效用进行早停，可将平均迭代次数减少约11%，同时保留自然停止所获最终答案质量的约98%。

Conclusion: Agentic RAG中大量迭代收益有限，可通过预测中间答案质量与效用实现有效早停，在几乎不损失最终答案质量的前提下提升效率。该框架为理解和优化Agentic RAG的迭代过程提供了新视角。

Abstract: Agentic Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has become a promising paradigm for multi-hop question answering, where a reasoning model iteratively issues queries to a retriever and incorporates newly retrieved context into subsequent reasoning steps. While this iterative process can improve final answer quality, current evaluations of agentic RAG largely focus on end-to-end outcomes and provide limited visibility into how a model's answer state changes during generation. In this work, we introduce an in-trajectory probing framework to study intermediate answer states in agentic RAG. Specifically, after each retrieval-reasoning iteration, we force an agentic model to stop reasoning and generate an intermediate answer based on its current state. This allows us to define two iteration-level measures: partial answer quality at each iteration, and partial utility as the change in partial answer quality across iterations. Our analysis across multi-hop QA benchmarks reveals that partial answer quality often plateaus before natural termination, with many later iterations contributing only small measurable improvements. Accordingly, we formulate two prediction tasks, partial answer quality prediction and partial utility prediction, and study trajectory-derived signals from intra-iteration, inter-iteration, and query-iteration perspectives. Experiments show that partial answer quality is more predictable than partial utility, with supervised models achieving Pearson's r above 0.43 for quality prediction. Finally, using predicted answer quality and utility for early stopping reduces average iteration count by about 11% while preserving about 98% of the final answer quality achieved by natural stopping.

</details>


### [378] [ReliGRec: Reliability-Oriented LLM-Based Generative Recommendation via User-Risk-Aware Prompt Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16560)
*Haoran Yang,Fei Chen,Yutian Xiao,Jiahao Liang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出 ReliGRec，一个面向 LLM 生成式推荐的弱监督可靠性导向框架，通过用户级弱风险代理标签在推理阶段选择不同 Prompt，将弱风险估计转化为生成时的控制信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实推荐系统中用户行为异质，部分用户偏好突变、重复交互或与协同邻域不一致，可能源于正常变化或恶意操纵（如 shilling attacks），但难以单独判定恶意意图。现有鲁棒推荐多在训练期重加权或图聚合中利用风险信号，而 LLM 生成式推荐中如何根据用户级弱风险自适应调整生成仍未被充分探索。

Method: ReliGRec 从评论反馈信号为部分用户构建用户级弱风险代理标签；用 Behavior Token 表示序列行为，用时间 Graph Token 表示协同上下文；通过 Dual-View Weak-Risk Estimator 融合表示生成用户级弱风险分数，并在推理时据此选择 Simple 或 Cautious Prompt。Cautious Prompt 鼓励关注稳定且协同支持的证据，减少对孤立、短期或重复交互的过度依赖。Behavior Token 通过弱风险估计与路由影响生成，聚合 Graph Token 为 next-item Semantic ID 生成提供协同上下文。

Result: 实验显示 ReliGRec 在推荐性能和弱风险代理标签预测上具有竞争力；路由分析刻画了弱风险引导提示在推荐质量与推理成本方面的行为。

Conclusion: ReliGRec 将弱风险估计从辅助预测任务转变为生成时的控制信号，实现基于用户级弱风险的提示路由，在 LLM 生成式推荐中平衡推荐质量与推理成本。

Abstract: User behavior in real-world recommender systems is heterogeneous. While some users exhibit coherent preferences, others show abrupt interest shifts, bursty interactions, excessive repetition, or inconsistency with collaborative neighborhoods. Such deviations may arise from benign variation or manipulation, including shilling attacks, but do not alone establish malicious intent. Existing robust recommenders exploit user-risk signals through training-time reweighting or graph aggregation, whereas adapting generation to estimated user-level weak risk remains underexplored in LLM-based generative recommendation. We propose ReliGRec (Reliability-oriented Generative Recommendation), a weakly supervised framework whose name denotes its design goal rather than a supervised reliability variable. ReliGRec derives user-level weak-risk proxy labels from review-feedback signals for a subset of users and represents sequential behavior and collaborative context using a Behavior Token and temporal Graph Tokens, respectively. A Dual-View Weak-Risk Estimator fuses the representations to produce a user-level weak-risk score that selects a Simple or Cautious Prompt at inference. The Cautious Prompt is designed to encourage attention to stable, collaboratively supported evidence while reducing overreliance on isolated, short-term, or repeated interactions. The Behavior Token affects generation through weak-risk estimation and routing, whereas the aggregated Graph Token provides collaborative context for next-item Semantic ID generation. ReliGRec thus turns weak-risk estimation from an auxiliary prediction into a generation-time control signal. Experiments report competitive recommendation and weak-risk proxy-label prediction, while routing analyses characterize the recommendation-quality and inference-cost behavior of weak-risk-guided prompting.

</details>


### [379] [Measuring Decision-Scale Use in Tool-Augmented LLMs: A Contrastive Urban Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16607)
*Ray Chen,Vivian Wong,Christan Grant*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出 URBANCONTRASTIVEQA 基准，检验工具增强语言模型能否进行“相对基线”的城市异常比较，而非简单比较原始计数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市决策关注某地活动是否相对其历史基线异常，而非原始数量大小；如安静社区 20 次接单可能比机场 180 次更异常。

Method: 构建 URBANCONTRASTIVEQA 基准，使用 NYC、Chicago、Seattle 公共出行数据配对城市情境并按偏离历史基线程度标注；评估 6 个指令微调模型、5 种工具输出格式。

Result: 仅给原始计数时，模型常选数值更大者，即使其对该区域并不更异常；服务器计算的基线分数和序数标签能提升准确率，但增益因模型而异。

Conclusion: 面向异构城市数据流，工具接口应暴露本地基线而非仅活动量；作者发布配对库、标签、评分脚本和数据卡。

Abstract: Urban decision-support often asks whether activity is unusually high or low for a specific place, not which place has the larger raw count. Twenty pickups in a quiet neighborhood can be more abnormal than 180 at an airport. We introduce URBANCONTRASTIVEQA, a benchmark that asks whether tool-augmented language models can make this baseline-relative comparison. Each item pairs two urban situations from public mobility data in NYC, Chicago, and Seattle, labeled by how far current activity deviates from that place's historical baseline. We evaluate six instruction-tuned models under five tool-output formats. With only raw counts, models often pick the larger number even when it is less abnormal for its zone. Server-computed baseline scores and ordinal labels raise accuracy, but gains vary by model. For heterogeneous urban feeds, tool interfaces need to expose local baselines, not just activity volumes. We release the pair bank, labels, scoring scripts, and data card.

</details>


### [380] [AURA: Agentic Diagnosis and Refinement for Production Recommender Systems at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16625)
*SungGeun Kim,Abhinav Narain,Daniel Nemirovsky*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出AURA，一个端到端agentic系统，用AI Agent在规模化下对推荐系统做质性诊断，并自动生成代码级改进，朝自改进推荐系统迈进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚合量化指标只能提供高层且不完整的画面，难以辨别推荐在何处、为何对真实用户表现不佳；而深入其质量与模式需要规模化的领域理解与客观推理。

Method: AURA使用专门化Agent读取生产参与日志（从数千到数百万会话），浮现推荐失败真实用户的模式与实例；再结合推荐系统自身代码、数据与训练流水线的上下文，提出并实现扎根代码库的改进。系统通过配置层引入各领域特定元素。

Result: 在一家大型媒体流媒体公司两个大型消费平台的生产数据上完成初步测试，涵盖系统设计、防护措施、运营经验与早期结果。

Conclusion: AURA弥合的诊断缺口并非流媒体独有，该架构可迁移，并具体映射到电商与在线零售推荐场景。

Abstract: How and why does a recommender system fail the users it serves? Oftentimes, practitioners are left to improve their algorithms based on a combination of feedback from stakeholder teams, domain expertise, and insights from data analyses. Yet the nuances of how and where recommendations perform well or poorly for end users are difficult to discern from aggregate quantitative metrics. Whereas these metrics provide a high-level and incomplete picture, further granularity into the quality of recommendations and their patterns requires reasoning with domain understanding and objectivity, at scale. We contemplate this complex conundrum and describe a method and implementation that uses the latest AI agentic advances to provide actionable diagnoses and improvements for production recommender systems. We present AURA (Agentic Understanding and Refinement of recommender Algorithms), an end-to-end agentic system that performs qualitative evaluation at scale and can then generate improvements to our algorithms at the code level. Specialized agents read production engagement logs, from thousands of sessions to millions, and surface patterns and examples of how the recommender fails real users. The next step uses those diagnoses and context about the recommender's own code, data, and training pipeline to propose and implement refinements grounded in that codebase. We report the system design, initial tests on production data from two large consumer platforms at a major media-streaming company, safeguards, operational learnings, and early results toward a self-improving recommender system. Finally, the diagnostic gap AURA closes is not specific to streaming. The architecture is built to transfer: every domain-specific element enters through the configuration layer that already ported it between our two platforms. We map it concretely to e-commerce and online-retail recommendation.

</details>


### [381] [Efficient Swing Computation for Retrieval in Large-Scale Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16850)
*Runhao Jiang,Renchi Yang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出ASC与K-ASC两种高效算法，用于近似与Top-K Swing查询，具有概率相对/加性误差理论保证，在八个真实数据集上实现数量级加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Swing分数计算方法因相对于物品度数的二次时间复杂度而代价高昂，或依赖截断启发式导致质量不佳，在数十亿交互的大图上不实用。

Method: ASC结合GNS与USS两种随机算法，自适应处理高度数与低度数查询物品；K-ASC采用过滤-精炼范式与精心设计的启发式策略，支持高效Top-K查询。

Result: 在八个真实数据集上，相比竞品实现数量级计算时间加速，同时保持相同的近似与Top-K查询质量；K-ASC在十亿边规模的Yambda和MAG数据集上仍高效。

Conclusion: ASC与K-ASC有效解决了Swing查询的可扩展性问题，兼顾效率与质量，适用于工业级大规模推荐系统。

Abstract: Given a user-item graph $G$, a query item $v_q$ and a target item $v_t$, the Swing score $sw(v_q, v_t)$ of the item pair $(v_q, v_t)$ leverages the user-item-user interaction structure to evaluate their similarity. This measure is found to be highly effective in item-to-item (i2i) retrieval task and finds extensive applications in industrial-scale recommender systems. However, existing solutions towards computing Swing scores are either prohibitively expensive due to their quadratic time complexity w.r.t. the item degree, or rely on truncation heuristics that yield unsatisfactory quality, rendering them impractical particularly on graphs with billions of interactions.
  In this paper, we present ASC and $K$-ASC, two novel and efficient algorithms for approximate and top-$K$ Swing queries, to address the aforementioned limitations. Specifically, these algorithms provide rigorous theoretical guarantees in probabilistic relative and additive errors of Swing values. The basic idea of ASC is to combine two randomized algorithms, GNS and USS, in a simple yet non-trivial way to adaptively process high- and low-degree query items with minimal runtime cost. In particular, $K$-ASC offers practical efficiency and effectiveness for top-$K$ queries through a filter-refinement paradigm with carefully-designed heuristics. Extensive experiments over eight real datasets demonstrate that ASC and $K$-ASC can achieve orders of magnitude speed-up over competitors in terms of computational time while offering the same approximate and top-$K$ query result quality, and in particular, $K$-ASC is highly efficient on massive graphs including the billion-edge Yambda and MAG datasets.

</details>


<div id='cs.GT'></div>

# cs.GT [[Back]](#toc)

### [382] [Symmetric solution of the Bellman optimality equation for repeated harmony game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16289)
*Hisato Komatsu*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 研究重复和谐博弈中Bellman最优方程的对称解，发现三类对称解，并通过强化学习数值考察智能体实际学到的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在社交困境博弈中，额外奖励或惩罚被用作促进合作的手段，因此有必要研究额外收益会改变博弈的理想情形（和谐博弈）。

Method: 求解重复和谐博弈的Bellman最优方程的对称解，对比其与All-C策略、囚徒困境的Win-stay Lose-shift策略的对应关系，并详细讨论非平凡策略行为；同时用强化学习算法数值考察智能体的学习结果。

Result: 计算表明存在三类对称解：一类对应平凡的All-C策略，另一类对应囚徒困境的Win-stay Lose-shift策略，最后一类对应具有非平凡行为的策略。

Conclusion: 重复和谐博弈存在多种对称最优解，其中包含已知策略及一种行为非平凡的新策略；强化学习实验揭示了智能体实际习得的策略。

Abstract: In social dilemma games, additional rewards or punishments have been studied as means of promoting cooperation. Therefore, it is important to investigate the ideal situation, in which such an additional payoff would change the game. In this study, we investigated the symmetric solution of the Bellman optimality equation for a repeated harmony game. The calculations showed that three types of symmetric solutions exist. One of them corresponds to the trivial All-C strategy, and another to the Win-stay Lose-shift strategy of the prisoners dilemma game. The nontrivial behavior of the strategy corresponding to the last solution is also discussed in detail. In addition, we numerically investigated which strategy the agents actually learn by the reinforcement learning algorithm.

</details>


### [383] [Auction Design with ROI-Constrained Bidders: Truthfulness and Revenue Maximization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16522)
*Zhiqiang Zhuang,Quan Yu,Yisong Wang,Kewen Wang,Zhe Wang*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究具有ROI约束投标者的真实且收益最大化拍卖，刻画了真实拍卖机制，并提出σ-increment机制及最优定价函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ROI约束在在线广告等拍卖中很关键，投标者不愿支付超过其获得价值固定比例的费用。现有研究需要理解当估值和ROI约束均为私有信息时，如何设计真实且收益最大化的拍卖。

Method: 首先刻画估值与ROI约束均私有时的真实拍卖，发现分配规则唯一决定支付规则。基于此，对多投标者提出类似Myerson最优机制的σ-increment机制；对单投标者，证明任意真实拍卖可被凸定价函数替代，并在估值或ROI约束公开时推导最优定价函数。

Result: σ-increment机制随σ趋近于零，在确定性真实机制中渐近最优；其收益至少达到所有真实机制最优期望收益的1/r̄比例（r̄为最大ROI约束）。单投标者情形下，凸定价函数对每种类型支付弱更高；分别得到估值或ROI约束公开时的最优定价函数。

Conclusion: 论文为ROI约束下的拍卖设计提供了理论刻画和机制构造，σ-increment机制具有渐近最优性和收益保证，单投标者最优定价函数也在两种公开信息情形下推导出来。

Abstract: The return-on-investment (ROI) constraint is central to many auctions, particularly in online advertising, where a bidder is unwilling to pay more than a fixed fraction of the value obtained. We study truthful and revenue-maximizing auctions for ROI-constrained bidders. We first characterize truthful auctions when both valuations and ROI constraints are private, showing that the allocation rule uniquely determines the payment rule. Building on this characterization, for multiple bidders we introduce $σ$-increment mechanisms that resemble Myerson's optimal mechanism~\cite{journals/mor/Myerson81}; as $σ$ vanishes, these mechanisms become asymptotically optimal among deterministic truthful mechanisms, and their revenue approaches at least a $1/\bar r$ fraction of the optimal expected revenue over all truthful mechanisms, where $\bar r$ is the largest possible ROI constraint. In the single-bidder setting, we prove that every truthful auction can be replaced by a convex pricing function with weakly higher payments for every type, and we derive the optimal pricing functions when either the valuation or the ROI constraint is public.

</details>


### [384] [Anchored Sequential Deliberation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16673)
*Sijing Tu,Ashish Goel*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文在Fain等人的序贯协商模型上引入"锚定"机制，研究一维决策空间中锚定强度λ对协商过程的收敛性与稳定性影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论仅把当前结果视为讨价还价的非协议选项，忽略了现有草案、政策或提案可能带来的社会影响和锚定效应。本文希望刻画锚定如何影响集体决策的协商动态。

Method: 引入锚定序贯协商：每轮随机选两人，先以强度λ向上一轮结果O_{t-1}靠拢，再以O_{t-1}为非协议选项进行纳什讨价还价，更新简化为O_t=(1-λ)Median{U,V,O_{t-1}}+λO_{t-1}。使用单调耦合、1-Wasserstein分析等工具。

Result: 证明收敛—稳定权衡：任意人群分布和λ<1下存在唯一平稳分布；1-Wasserstein收缩因子介于λ与(1+λ)/2之间，锚定越强混合越慢；平稳社会成本随λ弱递减，最坏失真为(1+√2)/2；识别唯一协商不动点，λ→1时平稳分布集中于该点；均匀人群的平稳失真介于1+(1-λ)/(9+7λ)与1+(1-λ)/(6(1+λ))之间，趋于1。

Conclusion: 锚定在减慢混合的同时能降低平稳失真、集中平稳分布。均匀与Beta人群的模拟结果验证了这些理论发现。

Abstract: Sequential deliberation is a mechanism for collective decision making: at each round, a uniformly randomly selected pair is asked to revise a collective outcome, which then becomes the reference point for the next round. Existing theory by Fain et al.~\cite{fain2017sequential} treats the current outcome solely as the disagreement alternative in bargaining. Yet an existing draft, policy, or proposal might carry social influence and anchor participants' expressed positions toward the status quo.
  We introduce anchored sequential deliberation on a one-dimensional decision space. In each round, two participants with bliss points $U$ and $V$ shift their positions toward the previous outcome $O_{t-1}$ with anchoring strength $λ$, then Nash-bargain using $O_{t-1}$ as the disagreement alternative. The update simplifies to $O_t=(1-λ)\mathsf{Median}\{U,V,O_{t-1}\}+λO_{t-1}$.
  We establish a convergence--stability trade-off. For every population distribution and $λ<1$, the process has a unique stationary distribution. A monotone coupling yields a $1$-Wasserstein contraction factor of at most $\frac{1+λ}{2}$ and at least $λ$; thus, stronger anchoring slows mixing. On the other hand, stationary social cost weakly decreases with $λ$, although the worst-case distortion remains $\frac{1+\sqrt{2}}{2}$. We also identify a unique \emph{deliberative fixed point}, where the expected unanchored movement is zero, and prove that the stationary distribution concentrates around it as $λ\to 1$. For the uniform population, stationary distortion lies between $1+\frac{1-λ}{9+7λ}$ and $1+\frac{1-λ}{6(1+λ)}$, with both bounds approaching $1$ as $λ\to1$. Simulations for uniform and Beta populations show that stronger anchoring slows mixing, concentrates the stationary distribution, and lowers stationary distortion in these instances.

</details>


### [385] [Constant Swap Regret in General-Sum Games via Optimistic Transition Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16751)
*Tung Mai*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出确定性、非耦合的学习动力学，在完全信息反馈下使有限多玩家一般和博弈达到独立于时间T的常数个体交换遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习动力学在一般和博弈中通常只能获得随T增长的遗憾，本文旨在实现常数（与T无关）的个体交换遗憾。

Method: 每个玩家预测偏离收益，用其更新行随机转移矩阵并采用平稳分布；证明结合利用平稳性的势函数论证、两尺度高阶预测分析及有根树表示，并给出对抗鲁棒变体（通用公共前缀切换包装）。

Result: n个玩家、每人至多m个动作时，各玩家个体交换遗憾为O(√n·m·logm·log^{5/2}(nm))；对抗变体保持自博弈界（至多差常数），对抗设置下个体交换遗憾≤7√(mT logm)。

Conclusion: 该工作首次（据摘要）实现确定性非耦合动力学在完全信息一般和博弈中的常数个体交换遗憾，并兼具对抗鲁棒性。

Abstract: We give deterministic and uncoupled learning dynamics for finite multiplayer general-sum games under full-information feedback that achieve constant individual swap regret, independent of the horizon $T$. With $n$ players and at most $m$ actions each, the individual swap regret of every player is $O(\sqrt{n} m \log m \log^{5/2}(nm))$ at every finite horizon. Each player predicts the deviation gains, then uses these predictions to update a row-stochastic transition matrix, and plays its stationary distribution. The proof combines a potential argument exploiting stationarity with a two-scale higher-order prediction analysis, using rooted-tree representations to handle the nonlinear dependence of deviation gains on the stationary distributions. An adversarially robust variant, obtained through a generic common-prefix switching wrapper, preserves the self-play bound up to a universal constant and guarantees individual swap regret at most $7\sqrt{m T \log m}$ in the adversarial setting.

</details>


### [386] [The price of anarchy in the max-distance network creation game is not constant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17395)
*Christoph Schlegel*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文构造了单边最大距离网络创建博弈中PoA为2^{Θ(√log n)}的无穷族纯Nash均衡，并证明多项式衰减边价下PoA为常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究单边最大距离网络创建博弈中无政府代价（PoA）的精确渐近界，特别是构造达到已知上界的下界实例。

Method: 在边价α=1时，对Lavrov-Loh-Messegué构造的大直径距离均匀图的二部双覆盖每条边进行细分，令每个细分顶点购买其两条关联边，通过距离计算排除所有有利的单边偏离。

Result: 构造出PoA≥2^{√log₂n−O(log log n)}的无穷族纯Nash均衡，结合已知上界得到2^{Θ(√log n)}；该均衡非严格；并简证多项式衰减边价下PoA为常数。

Conclusion: 确定了单边最大距离网络创建博弈沿构造序列的PoA紧界为2^{Θ(√log n)}，且多项式衰减边价下PoA保持常数。

Abstract: At edge price $α=1$, we construct an infinite family of pure Nash equilibria of the unilateral max-distance network creation game with $\PoA\ge2^{\sqrt{\log_2 n}-O(\log\log n)}$. Together with the known upper bound, this gives $2^{Θ(\sqrt{\log n})}$ along the constructed sequence of population sizes. We subdivide every edge of the bipartite double cover of a distance-uniform graph with large diameter constructed by Lavrov, Loh and Messegué, and let each subdivision vertex buy its two incident edges. A distance calculation rules out every profitable unilateral deviation. The equilibria are not strict. We also give a short proof that the price of anarchy is constant for every polynomially vanishing edge price.

</details>


### [387] [On testing the incentive compatibility of single-parameter allocation mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17406)
*Jason Milionis,William Pires*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 首次将博弈论与性质测试结合，研究高效测试分配机制的激励相容性（IC），针对单参数分配规则和定价函数给出查询复杂度的算法与下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 判断机制是否激励相容（IC）在博弈论中很重要，但高效测试该性质此前未被研究，本文开创新方向。

Method: 借鉴布尔函数单调性测试，构造离散单参数分配规则的测试器；首次研究超网格上向量值函数的单调性测试；同时对定价函数扩展上界，克服价格与分配规则相互依赖变化的挑战。

Result: 给出 Õ(n/ε)-查询算法测试 n 玩家分配机制的逐坐标单调性；证明匹配下界 Ω̃(n/ε)，即使在布尔超立方体或超网格、双向测试且自适应查询下仍成立；定价函数达到相同查询复杂度。

Conclusion: 在博弈论与性质测试交叉领域取得首个成果，为高效测试IC提供算法与下界，并扩展到定价函数。

Abstract: This paper is the first work at the intersection of game theory and property testing, giving algorithms and lower bounds for efficiently testing whether an allocation mechanism is incentive compatible (IC). We propose distinguishing whether a mechanism is $ε$-far from being IC, i.e., when it observes many monotonicity "violations." Conceptually, inspired by the literature on Boolean function monotonicity testing, we construct a tester for discrete single-parameter allocation rules. Technically, our work is the first to consider monotonicity testing of vector-valued functions on the hypergrid. We give a $\tilde{O}(n/ε)$-query algorithm to test whether a function (representing n-player allocation mechanisms) is coordinate-wise monotone versus $ε$-far from it. We also show a matching lower bound: the class of coordinate-wise monotone vector-valued functions on a Boolean hypercube or hypergrid requires $\tildeΩ(n/ε)$ queries to test whether it is $ε$-far from monotonicity, and this holds even if the tester is two-sided and allowed to make adaptive queries. Finally, we extend our upper bound to and give a tester of the same query complexity for pricing functions of allocation mechanisms. This requires overcoming the technical challenge that the path in function space to the closest IC mechanism may involve interdependent changes to both the price and the allocation rule.

</details>
